| RD-532 | V1 public unsupported diagnostic logical contract | OWNER-APPROVED / PUBLIC-UNSUPPORTED-DIAGNOSTIC-BOUNDARY-CLOSED / EXACT-PRODUCT-CODE-OPEN / PRODUCT-OPEN | Official ingress distinguishes MALFORMED, UNKNOWN, KNOWN_UNSUPPORTED and PROFILE_CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED semantic failure classes, with the last admitting reason-specific projection for a known identity rejected by target/profile capability. These are logical classes, not fixed product enums, numeric codes, wire spellings or localized messages. Failure is decided at the earliest authoritative BYTE_INGRESS, PARSE, TYPED_ADMISSION, CAPABILITY_ADMISSION or EXECUTION phase, with stable semantic identity, SourceEpoch, target/profile, span where available, reason and provenance sufficient for deterministic evidence. Unsupported input never silently falls back, host-infers or partially publishes; exact public diagnostic registry remains product-owned. |

## RD-533 — zero-latency logical-tick reaction boundary

근거: [DECISIONS L3270](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-533 | zero-latency logical-tick reaction boundary | OWNER-APPROVED / ZERO-LATENCY-REACTION-CLOSURE-CLOSED / PRODUCT-OPEN | Finite zero-latency work settles inside one logical madi's deterministic reaction closure. Work emitted by reaction r first becomes eligible at r+1 and same-stack direct reentry is forbidden. Only dependency relations own causal partial order; source, registration, worker, completion and traversal order have no semantic authority. Stable identity canonizes enumeration, receipts and replay but grants no priority. Equal DAGs yield equal result, state, failure and receipt digest independent of traversal and worker count. Static zero-delay cycles fail admission; dynamic cycles or versioned reaction, causal-depth, WorkCost, pending-event or PatchCandidate ceiling violations abort the whole current-madi candidate without truncation or deferral. Enabled work produces PatchCandidate values, and conflict/failure prevents partial state or outbox publication. |

## RD-534 — strict source ingress decoder and physical-versus-semantic source identity

근거: [DECISIONS L3271](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-534 | strict source ingress decoder and physical-versus-semantic source identity | OWNER-APPROVED / STRICT-UTF8-INGRESS-CLOSED / RD-477-CLAUSE-CORRECTED / PRODUCT-OPEN | Each SourceEpochIdentity binds exactly one DecoderProfileIdentity. V1 admits strict UTF-8 only and forbids alternate-encoding detection, UTF-8 BOM, BOM stripping, replacement insertion, locale/codepage fallback, editor metadata authority and content sniffing. Structurally invalid UTF-8 fails at byte ingress. Structural newline accepts LF, CRLF and EOF; CR belongs only to a CRLF pair and lone CR is not a bootstrap terminator. This successor corrects only RD-477's terminator clause while retaining raw-byte-zero and registered-language constraints. Raw 글무늬 keeps its separate CRLF/lone-CR body normalization owner. PhysicalSourceByteIdentity records exact bytes and provenance while decoded/semantic identity records authorized processing; LF and CRLF may differ physically yet share structural meaning, and receipts preserve both roles. Exact hashes, fields and wire remain product-owned. |

## RD-535 — GOER-R1-U1 guard outcome, selection and evidence-only emission

근거: [DECISIONS L3272](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-535 | GOER-R1-U1 guard outcome, selection and evidence-only emission | OWNER-APPROVED / GUARD-SELECTION-SEMANTICS-CLOSED / EXACT-DIAGNOSTIC-AND-WIRE-OPEN / PRODUCT-OPEN | One settled InputSnapshot evaluates the complete canonical eligible TransitionIdentity set without source-order, completion-order or first-wins authority. GUARD_FALSE disables only that transition. GUARD_UNRESOLVED, GUARD_FAILURE and host/internal fault abort the entire selection set. Zero true guards is normal NO_TRANSITION; exactly one yields one atomic state-change-plus-action candidate; two or more fail closed without selection. Guard outcomes are receipt evidence and, when needed, diagnostic evidence only: they are not semantic events, actions, world mutations, patches, outbox entries or capability authority. Guard evaluation, failure and abort publish no partial state, action or outbox. Exact diagnostic code, registry schema and wire remain product-owned and OPEN. |

UTF-16 offsets, line/column, Unicode-scalar and grapheme-cluster coordinates are deterministic projections from that authoritative byte coordinate and are not independent semantic or source authority. ## RD-546 — JSM-U3 lifecycle binding

근거: [DECISIONS L3484](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

Pause admits and evaluates no new delivery and buffers no hidden input; an input arriving while paused must be rejected or handled by a separately explicit upstream contract. Resume continues the same instance from its last committed state and does not reinitialize or replay hidden input. Stop prevents further delivery and seals the final lifecycle evidence. Automatic restart, retry, timeout and rebinding of an already-existing instance are not implied. Exact lifecycle enum spelling, physical storage and authored syntax remain OPEN. |

## RD-582 — External request response two-reaction protocol

근거: [DECISIONS L3935](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

Pre-commit external calls, same-reaction response success and treating irreversible external side effects as rollbackable transaction results are forbidden. RD-561~562 integrated dependency resolution and its single external adapter/boundary contract identity slot are reused without canonical-name closure or competing identity families. |

## RD-584 — ExecutionRelation Wave 1 success-only completion and ordering gates

근거: [DECISIONS L3969](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

The three existing relations remain distinct: SEQUENCE `~고` requires start(A) before start(B); AFTER_COMPLETION `~고서` requires successful complete(A) before start(B); AFTER_PUBLISH `~고 나서` requires successful publish(A) before start(B). If the required predecessor event does not occur, B admission is zero. Source, scheduler, worker, completion or physical timing order is non-authority. No new event identity, source syntax, scheduler identity or wall-clock authority is created. |

## RD-585 — ExecutionRelation Wave 1 structured-group failure and committed-state boundary

AFTER_COMPLETION permits `A complete -> B start -> A publish reject/failure`; the same cancel/join/discard boundary applies. AFTER_PUBLISH starts B only after successful A publish; later B failure cannot retroactively roll back A committed state, and correction requires explicit compensation. RD-286 separate relation family is not all twelve active ExecutionRelations; RD-284 SelectionRelation and exact override-family attribution remain FUTURE_WAVE. In Jjaim, RD-581 outer atomic commit remains the containing visibility owner and Wave 1 creates no independent child publish. |

## RD-588 — STDLIB V1 deterministic RNG exact profile

근거: [DECISIONS L4043](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-588 | STDLIB V1 deterministic RNG exact profile | OWNER-BOUND / SEMANTIC-CLOSED / PRODUCT-OPEN | The V1 deterministic simulation profile is `DDN-RNG-v1/ChaCha12`, not a cryptographic API claim. State contains ChaCha constants, eight little-endian seed words, a 64-bit block counter and 64-bit RandomStreamIdentity; output is sixteen little-endian u32 words after six double-rounds. Admitted seed forms are SeedBytes32 and SeedU64, with SeedU64 expanded by SHA-256 of the fixed domain string plus U64_LE. Public position is block counter plus word index, wrap is forbidden, and every operation requires an explicit positive word budget. Bool, bounded rejection sampling, choice, descending Fisher-Yates and exact rational grid projection have the fixed consumption rules. Hidden entropy, fallback, schedule or host ordering and hidden reservoirs are non-authority. V-RNG-01~05 are fixed discrimination vectors; product execution and executable oracle remain zero. |

## RD-589 — STDLIB V1 canonical solver method matrix

근거: [DECISIONS L4044](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-589 | STDLIB V1 canonical solver method matrix | OWNER-BOUND / SEMANTIC-CLOSED / PRODUCT-OPEN | The V1 canonical matrix is exact Brent-Dekker for bracketed scalar roots, classical fixed-step RK4 for non-stiff ODE, composite Simpson with positive even partition for definite integration, and deterministic partial-pivot LU/Gaussian elimination for square linear systems. Evaluation, branch, accumulation and pivot tie order are fixed by the source-owner material. Automatic method selection, fallback, hidden endpoint adjustment and backend-dependent semantic results are forbidden. Unsupported stiff ODE and multivariate nonlinear systems fail explicitly. Failure, count, branch trace, termination and backend descriptor remain distinct receipt fields; physical API, registry and implementation remain OPEN. |

## RD-590 — STDLIB solver Fixed64 numeric profile and authority vectors

근거: [DECISIONS L4045](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-590 | STDLIB solver Fixed64 numeric profile and authority vectors | OWNER-BOUND / SEMANTIC-CLOSED / PHYSICAL-PUBLIC-WIRE-OPEN / PRODUCT-OPEN | `DDN-SOLVER-FIXED64-V1` version 1 is signed Q31.32 over a signed 64-bit two's-complement raw value with 32 fractional bits, ULP 2^-32 and canonical corpus scalar encoding as exactly eight little-endian bytes. Admission and every precision-loss boundary use checked roundTiesToEven. Addition and subtraction are checked raw operations; multiplication and division use exact widened intermediates before ties-to-even quantization. Division by zero, overflow and unrepresentable conversion keep distinct numeric-owner failures. Binary64, FMA, extended precision, reassociation, saturation and truncation are non-authority. Brent representable resolution is one raw ULP. The fixed solver corpus is BR-01~06, RK-01~02, SM-01~02 and LU-01~02, 12/12 source-owner vectors; this is conformance material, not a public wire claim. |

PRESENT requires a value, ABSENT means authoritative optional absence, NOT_PRODUCED means failure produced no artifact; Native/WASM/Workbench compare every field except surface exactly. ## RD-665 — Subject identity persistent physical-owner contract

근거: [DECISIONS L5726](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-665 | Subject identity persistent physical-owner contract | OWNER-GATED CANDIDATE / SUBJECT-IDENTITY-PHYSICAL-OWNER-R1-U1 / PHYSICAL-CONTRACT / NOT-PRODUCT | SUBJECT-IDENTITY-PHYSICAL-O1=A binds persistent WorldSpaceIdentity and SubjectIdentity carriers inside the existing Subject/world semantic owner with NEW_SEMANTIC_IDENTITY=0. The shared world owner issues an exact 32-byte CSPRNG nonce and computes SHA-256 over domain DDN-WORLD-SPACE-IDENTITY-V1 followed by NUL, U16BE(1), field count 1, tag 0x01, U32BE(32), and the nonce. The shared subject owner atomically allocates the next U64BE ordinal and computes SHA-256 over domain DDN-SUBJECT-IDENTITY-V1 followed by NUL, U16BE(1), field count 2, tag 0x01 plus the 32-byte WorldSpaceIdentity, and tag 0x02 plus U32BE(8) and the ordinal. R1 generation is zero, reuse is forbidden and tombstones are retained. The maximum allocatable ordinal is u64::MAX - 1; successful publication advances next_subject_ordinal to u64::MAX, which is a terminal nonallocating state, so ordinal u64::MAX is never allocated. Abort causes no advance or publication. PESI, HistoryRevisionIdentity, state_hash, EntityId, Capability, path, time, machine and adapter-local counters are not authority. Saved-world custody records the exact world identity, next ordinal, generation and tombstone set; its integrity digest is nonsemantic. Native, WASM and Workbench use one shared issuer/verifier/resolver. Missing, malformed, duplicate, wrong-version, foreign, stale, reused, exhausted or mismatched custody fails closed while preserving OLD_COMPLETE and pre-state with partial publication zero. Product landing, diagnostics/schema/wire, pack/oracle, parity and VC04 complete-row remain OPEN; complete REQUIRED remains 11/28 with 17 blockers and protected transitions zero. |

## RD-696 — C3 입력 조회 비소비 원칙

근거: [DECISIONS L6665](SOURCE_AUTHORITY.md#source-0001)

| RD-696 | C3 입력 조회 비소비 원칙 | CANDIDATE / NOT CURRENT / C3-INPUT-OBSERVE-NONCONSUMING-R1-U1 / PRODUCT-OPEN | admitted logical input event의 조회는 관찰이다. 동일 admitted snapshot과 동일한 명시 조회 조건에 대한 반복적인 성공 조회는 동일한 logical event 자료와 기존의 logical order를 본다. 조회는 사건 목록, logical ordinal, 공유된 논리 cursor 또는 다른 조회자가 볼 자료를 consume/delete/ack/dequeue하지 않으며 세계를 변경하지 않는다. 사건 소비·ack·dequeue가 필요하면 별도의 명시 계약이 필요하고 조회로부터 암묵적으로 도출하지 않는다.  유효한 admitted snapshot에 정상 사건이 0개이면 empty typed collection이다. 필수 snapshot/event 구조의 누락·손상은 실패이며 정상 empty로 바꾸지 않는다. 숨은 first/last 선택, 조회 순서에 따른 winner, host polling으로 logical input을 대체하는 경로를 허용하지 않는다. 이 규범은 shared semantic cursor를 대상으로 한다. explicit query 조건이나 world authority를 만들지 않는 reader-private iterator/local view 상태를 일률적으로 금지하지 않는다. 기존 RD-283~284의 명시 message delivery/FIFO/rendezvous 소비·hand-off 의미는 보존한다. RD-293·445의 provider fan-out, RD-633~639의 logical input/order/integrity, RD-261·282·286의 기존 snapshot·물리 입력 경계를 참조하며 기존 본문을 수정하지 않는다.  C3 일반 조회 규범 하나만 결속한다. C4는 RD-138·556 설명 crosswalk로 보존하며 새 RD를 만들지 않는다. 알림씨·Action·logical event identity를 합치지 않는다. 새 query 이름·등록 표면·DefinitionIdentity·ParamPinIdentity·반환 schema·진단·wire·budget, 통합 입력 기록 형식이나 조작 반응 bridge를 배정하지 않는다. Subject/atom·Tuple·RNG·FRAME2 기존 계약도 변경하지 않는다.  고정 설계 검토는 PASS_TARGET_BOUND / DESIGN_ONLY이며 당시 OWNER_ADOPTION PENDING은 후속 ②가 방향 승인 및 이번 한정 결속 지시로 연결한다. 설계 원문과 검토는 불변 보존한다. 그 PASS를 candidate/product PASS로 상속하지 않는다. 후보 독립검토는 predecessor 보존·승인 범위 투영·조립·manifest/seal/custody 변경분만 대상으로 한다.  제품 query 구현·Native/WASM/CLI/Workbench 검증, physical pack/executable oracle, complete-row, support/readiness/maturity, release/Human 상태는 전이하지 않는다. 공식18/28·blockers10 및 public SPEC v0.8.1 유지. WO19 장기 진행과 자동화는 정지 유지. 최종 Owner 승인 전 CURRENT 전환 금지. |
